## Understand terms
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for managing your mutual fund investments. It is the total percentage of a company's fund assets used for administrative, management, advertising, and other expenses.
A percentage of your capital gains payable to the government upon exiting your mutual fund investments. Taxation is categorized as long-term capital gains (LTCG) and short-term capital gains (STCG) depending on your holding period and the type of fund.
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for exiting a fund (fully or partially) before the completion of a specified period from the date of investment.
A form of tax payable for the purchase or sale of an asset or security.

## Understand terms
HDFC Large Cap Fund Direct Growth is a Equity Mutual Fund Scheme launched by HDFC Mutual Fund. This scheme was made available to investors on 10 Dec 1999. Rahul Baijal is the Current Fund Manager of HDFC Large Cap Fund Direct Growth fund. The fund currently has an Asset Under Management(AUM) of ₹9,86,237 Cr and the Latest NAV as of 28 Sep 2026 is ₹1,170.11. The HDFC Large Cap Fund Direct Growth is rated Very High risk. Minimum SIP Investment is set to ₹100. Minimum Lumpsum Investment is ₹100. Exit load of 1% if redeemed within 1 year ;
The scheme seeks to provide long-term capital appreciation/income by investing predominantly in Large-Cap companies.
Source: https://groww.in/mutual-funds/hdfc-large-cap-fund-direct-growth (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## Understand terms
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for managing your mutual fund investments. It is the total percentage of a company's fund assets used for administrative, management, advertising, and other expenses.
A percentage of your capital gains payable to the government upon exiting your mutual fund investments. Taxation is categorized as long-term capital gains (LTCG) and short-term capital gains (STCG) depending on your holding period and the type of fund.
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for exiting a fund (fully or partially) before the completion of a specified period from the date of investment.
A form of tax payable for the purchase or sale of an asset or security.
Source: https://groww.in/mutual-funds/hdfc-equity-fund-direct-growth (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## Understand terms
Exit load of 1% if redeemed within 1 year
from July 1st 2020
If you redeem within one year, returns are taxed at 20%. If you redeem after one year, returns exceeding Rs 1.25 lakh in a financial year are taxed at 12.5%.

## Understand terms
HDFC Flexi Cap Direct Plan Growth is a Equity Mutual Fund Scheme launched by HDFC Mutual Fund. This scheme was made available to investors on 10 Dec 1999. Dhruv Muchhal is the Current Fund Manager of HDFC Flexi Cap Direct Plan Growth fund. The fund currently has an Asset Under Management(AUM) of ₹9,86,237 Cr and the Latest NAV as of 28 Sep 2026 is ₹2,181.07. The HDFC Flexi Cap Direct Plan Growth is rated Very High risk. Minimum SIP Investment is set to ₹100. Minimum Lumpsum Investment is ₹100. Exit load of 1% if redeemed within 1 year ;
The scheme seeks to generate capital appreciation / income from a portfolio, predominantly invested in equity & equity related instruments.
Source: https://groww.in/mutual-funds/hdfc-equity-fund-direct-growth (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## Understand terms
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for managing your mutual fund investments. It is the total percentage of a company's fund assets used for administrative, management, advertising, and other expenses.
A percentage of your capital gains payable to the government upon exiting your mutual fund investments. Taxation is categorized as long-term capital gains (LTCG) and short-term capital gains (STCG) depending on your holding period and the type of fund.
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for exiting a fund (fully or partially) before the completion of a specified period from the date of investment.
A form of tax payable for the purchase or sale of an asset or security.
01 Jan 2013 --
01 Jan 2013
--
Nil
from July 1st 2020
If you redeem within one year, returns are taxed at 20%. If you redeem after one year, returns exceeding Rs 1.25 lakh in a financial year are taxed at 12.5%.

## Understand terms
HDFC ELSS Tax Saver Fund Direct Plan Growth is a Equity Mutual Fund Scheme launched by HDFC Mutual Fund. This scheme was made available to investors on 10 Dec 1999. Amar Kalkundrikar is the Current Fund Manager of HDFC ELSS Tax Saver Fund Direct Plan Growth fund. The fund currently has an Asset Under Management(AUM) of ₹9,86,237 Cr and the Latest NAV as of 28 Sep 2026 is ₹1,426.93. The HDFC ELSS Tax Saver Fund Direct Plan Growth is rated Very High risk. Minimum SIP Investment is set to ₹500. Minimum Lumpsum Investment is ₹500. ;
The scheme seeks to generate capital appreciation / income from a portfolio, comprising predominantly of equity & equity related instruments.
Source: https://groww.in/mutual-funds/hdfc-elss-tax-saver-fund-direct-plan-growth (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## Understand terms

## Understand terms
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for managing your mutual fund investments. It is the total percentage of a company's fund assets used for administrative, management, advertising, and other expenses.
A percentage of your capital gains payable to the government upon exiting your mutual fund investments. Taxation is categorized as long-term capital gains (LTCG) and short-term capital gains (STCG) depending on your holding period and the type of fund.
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for exiting a fund (fully or partially) before the completion of a specified period from the date of investment.
A form of tax payable for the purchase or sale of an asset or security.
Source: https://groww.in/mutual-funds/hdfc-small-cap-fund-direct-growth (retrieved 2026-09-30)

from July 1st 2020

## Understand terms
HDFC Small Cap Fund Direct Growth is a Equity Mutual Fund Scheme launched by HDFC Mutual Fund. This scheme was made available to investors on 10 Dec 1999. Dhruv Muchhal is the Current Fund Manager of HDFC Small Cap Fund Direct Growth fund. The fund currently has an Asset Under Management(AUM) of ₹9,86,237 Cr and the Latest NAV as of 28 Sep 2026 is ₹157.26. The HDFC Small Cap Fund Direct Growth is rated Very High risk. Minimum SIP Investment is set to ₹100. Minimum Lumpsum Investment is ₹100. Exit load of 1% if redeemed within 1 year ;
The scheme seeks to provide long-term capital appreciation /income by investing predominantly in Small-Cap companies.
Source: https://groww.in/mutual-funds/hdfc-small-cap-fund-direct-growth (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## Understand terms
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for managing your mutual fund investments. It is the total percentage of a company's fund assets used for administrative, management, advertising, and other expenses.
A percentage of your capital gains payable to the government upon exiting your mutual fund investments. Taxation is categorized as long-term capital gains (LTCG) and short-term capital gains (STCG) depending on your holding period and the type of fund.
A fee payable to a mutual fund house for exiting a fund (fully or partially) before the completion of a specified period from the date of investment.
A form of tax payable for the purchase or sale of an asset or security.
Source: https://groww.in/mutual-funds/hdfc-balanced-advantage-fund-direct-growth (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## Understand terms
HDFC Balanced Advantage Fund Direct Growth is a Hybrid Mutual Fund Scheme launched by HDFC Mutual Fund. This scheme was made available to investors on 10 Dec 1999. Anil Bamboli is the Current Fund Manager of HDFC Balanced Advantage Fund Direct Growth fund. The fund currently has an Asset Under Management(AUM) of ₹9,86,237 Cr and the Latest NAV as of 28 Sep 2026 is ₹551.04. The HDFC Balanced Advantage Fund Direct Growth is rated Very High risk. Minimum SIP Investment is set to ₹100. Minimum Lumpsum Investment is ₹100. Exit Load for units in excess of 15% of the investment,1% will be charged for redemption within 1 year. ;
The Scheme seeks to provide long term capital appreciation / income from a dynamic mix of equity and debt investments.

## About HDFC Large Cap Fund
An open ended equity scheme predominantly investing in large cap stocks. This is a simple and performing scheme which is eligible for distribution by new cadre of distributors. The Scheme aims to provide long-term capital appreciation. It will maintain a minimum exposure of 80% to Large Cap stocks, as defined by SEBI, which currently are the 100 largest companies in India in terms of full market capitalization. Generally, large cap companies are well established businesses that are in operation for a longer period of time and are well positioned to absorb business pressures across various market cycles. Due to their size, they also benefit from economies of scale. The Scheme will remain diversified across key sectors and economic variables.

## About HDFC Large Cap Fund
An open ended equity scheme predominantly investing in large cap stocks.
This is a simple and performing scheme which is eligible for distribution by new cadre of distributors.
The Scheme aims to provide long-term capital appreciation. It will maintain a minimum exposure of 80% to Large Cap stocks, as defined by SEBI, which currently are the 100 largest companies in India in terms of full market capitalization. Generally, large cap companies are well established businesses that are in operation for a longer period of time and are well positioned to absorb business pressures across various market cycles. Due to their size, they also benefit from economies of scale.
The Scheme will remain diversified across key sectors and economic variables.

## About HDFC Large Cap Fund
Entry Load An entry load is a fee charged to investors when they purchase units of a Mutual Fund scheme. This fee was used to cover distribution and marketing expenses incurred by the Asset Management Company (AMC). Currently, Mutual Funds in India do not levy entry loads. NA TER TER The Total Expense Ration (TER) represents the costs for running and managing a Mutual Fund scheme. It is calculated as a percentage of the scheme"s average Net Asset Value (NAV). The daily NAV of a Mutual Fund is disclosed after deducting these expenses. Disclaimer Including Additional Expenses and Goods and Service Tax on Management Fees, if any. ## Exit Load
● In respect of each purchase/switch-in of Units, an Exit Load of 1.00% is payable if Units are redeemed/switched-out within 1 year from the date of allotment. ● No Exit Load is payable if Units are redeemed/switched-out after 1 year from the date of allotment. No Entry/Exit Load shall be levied on bonus units and units allotted on dividend reinvestment
Source: https://www.hdfcfund.com/explore/mutual-funds/hdfc-large-cap-fund/direct (retrieved 2026-09-30)

### How to Invest in HDFC Large Cap Fund?
Investors can apply for their transactions requests either offline or electronically using the relevant application / transaction request forms available on our website or at any of our Officials Points of Acceptance.
The application form/transaction slip for subscription/ redemption/ switches can be submitted at our Official Points of Acceptances whose addresses are available on the website of the AMC. These include:
AMC / RTA"s branches i.e. Investor Services Centres*
HDFC MF website and App/ RTA website for Investors to transact
MFSS/BSE StAR MF/NMF II platforms of the Stock Exchanges(s)
Authorized Points of Service of MF Utilities India Private Limited (MFUI)
Channel partners/ Distributors/ RIAs/ Portfolio Managers/Execution Only Platforms (EOPs) who have tied up with the AMC
MF Central
* Note: Business Centres i.e. Sales offices of HDFC AMC are not Official Points of Acceptance of transactions. The above list is indicative. For further details, including cut-off timing and applicability of NAV, refer Section II. Please refer Scheme Documents above for more details
Ask Me

## About HDFC Flexi Cap Fund
An open ended dynamic equity scheme investing across large cap, mid cap & small cap stocks
30+ Years of Growth & Trust
Since inception, HDFC Flexi Cap Fund has navigated multiple market cycle over more than 3 decades Flexi Cap Funds offer fund managers complete flexibility to allocate assets across large, mid, and small-cap stocks, based on market conditions. This adaptability makes HDFC Flexi Cap Fund attractive to investors seeking diversified exposure to the equity market.
Since inception, HDFC Flexi Cap Fund has navigated multiple market cycle over more than 3 decades
Flexi Cap Funds offer fund managers complete flexibility to allocate assets across large, mid, and small-cap stocks, based on market conditions. This adaptability makes HDFC Flexi Cap Fund attractive to investors seeking diversified exposure to the equity market.

## What is HDFC Flexi Cap Fund?
Fund Description: HDFC Flexi Cap Fund is an open-ended equity mutual fund scheme that invests across large-cap, mid-cap, and small-cap companies.
This allows the fund to combine:
Stability from established market leaders
Growth potential from mid-sized companies
Emerging opportunities from smaller businesses
helping create a balanced approach to long-term wealth creation.

## How to start investing
Investors can start investing in HDFC Flexi Cap Fund through the following steps:
Complete your KYC Keep PAN, Aadhaar and bank details ready.
Visit HDFC Mutual Fund Website / App Go to the fund page and click Invest Now.
Choose Investment Mode SIP - invest a fixed amount monthly Lump Sum - invest one time
Select Plan & Option Direct or Regular Plan Growth or IDCW option
Enter Amount & Payment Details Set SIP date or make payment online.
Confirm & Start Investing Your investment will be processed and units will be allotted.

## How to start investing
Entry Load An entry load is a fee charged to investors when they purchase units of a Mutual Fund scheme. This fee was used to cover distribution and marketing expenses incurred by the Asset Management Company (AMC). Currently, Mutual Funds in India do not levy entry loads. NA TER TER The Total Expense Ration (TER) represents the costs for running and managing a Mutual Fund scheme. It is calculated as a percentage of the scheme"s average Net Asset Value (NAV). The daily NAV of a Mutual Fund is disclosed after deducting these expenses. Disclaimer Including Additional Expenses and Goods and Service Tax on Management Fees, if any. Click here to view the Total Expense Ratio 0.77 Lock in A lock-in period is the fixed duration during which investors cannot sell or redeem their investments/Mutual Fund units. For example, Equity Linked Savings Schemes (ELSS) Funds have a 3-year lock-in. NA

## Exit Load
In respect of each purchase / switch-in of Units, an Exit Load of 1.00% is payable if Units are redeemed / switched-out within 1 year from the date of allotment.

### What is HDFC Flexi Cap Fund?
HDFC Flexi Cap Fund is an open-ended equity mutual fund that invests across large-cap, mid-cap, and small-cap stocks, giving the fund manager flexibility to capture opportunities across market segments.

## FAQs
The minimum SIP amount for HDFC Flexi Cap Fund is ₹100 (or as specified by the scheme). Investors can start a Systematic Investment Plan with small periodic investments and increase the amount over time based on their financial goals. You can refer website or the scheme information document for the latest investment requirements.

## About HDFC ELSS - Tax Saver Fund
The scheme offers the dual benefit of tax saving and wealth creation. It comes with a lock-in period of three years and provides individuals/HUFs a deduction from gross total income for investments in Equity-Linked Savings Scheme upto ₹1.5 lakh under section 80C of the Income Tax Act 1961.
The fund invests 80-100% of its portfolio in equity or equity related instruments.The equity strategy aims to provide long term capital appreciation from a reasonably diversified portfolio consisting of mid cap and large cap companies which:
are likely to achive above average growth than the industry,
enjoy distinct competetive advantages,
have superior financial strenghts.
Source: https://www.hdfcfund.com/explore/mutual-funds/hdfc-elss-tax-saver-fund/direct (retrieved 2026-09-30)

Entry Load An entry load is a fee charged to investors when they purchase units of a Mutual Fund scheme. This fee was used to cover distribution and marketing expenses incurred by the Asset Management Company (AMC). Currently, Mutual Funds in India do not levy entry loads. NA TER TER The Total Expense Ration (TER) represents the costs for running and managing a Mutual Fund scheme. It is calculated as a percentage of the scheme"s average Net Asset Value (NAV). The daily NAV of a Mutual Fund is disclosed after deducting these expenses. Disclaimer Including Additional Expenses and Goods and Service Tax on Management Fees, if any. ### 1. How to Invest in HDFC ELSS Tax Saver?
Investors can apply for their transactions requests either offline or electronically using the relevant application / transaction request forms available on our website or at any of our Officials Points of Acceptance. The application form/transaction slip for subscription/ redemption/ switches can be submitted at our Official Points of Acceptances whose addresses are available on the website of the AMC. These include: 1. AMC / RTA"s branches i.e. Investor Services Centres* 2. HDFC MF website and App/ RTA website for Investors to transact 3. MFSS/BSE StAR MF/NMF II platforms of the Stock Exchanges(s) 4. Authorized Points of Service of MF Utilities India Private Limited (MFUI) 5. Channel partners/ Distributors/ RIAs/ Portfolio Managers/Execution Only Platforms (EOPs) who have tied up with the AMC 6. MF Central * Note: Business Centres i.e. Sales offices of HDFC AMC are not Official Points of Acceptance of transactions. Please refer offer documents for more details.

### 2. Can I invest in SIP and Lump Sum of the HDFC ELSS Tax Saver?
Yes, SIP as well as Lump Sum investments are permitted with the minimum application amount being ₹500. Any additional purchase must have a minimum amount of ₹500. The SIP and Lumpsum are available in direct and regular plan with both Growth and IDCW option.

block_kind    : faq
chunk_index   : 41   token_count: 164   chars: 11797-12726

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### 3. How to Redeem HDFC ELSS Tax Saver?
Units of the scheme can be redeemed/switched out on any Business day at NAV based prices. For Redemption (including switch-out) applications • In respect of valid applications received upto 3 p.m. on a Business Day by the Fund, same day's closing NAV shall be applicable. • In respect of valid applications received after 3 p.m. on a Business Day by the Fund, the closing NAV of the next Business Day shall be applicable. Transactions through online facilities / electronic modes: The time of transaction done through various online facilities / electronic modes offered by the AMC, for the purpose of determining the applicability of NAV, would be the time when the request for purchase / sale / switch of units is received in the servers of AMC/RTA. The AMC has the right to amend cut off timings subject to SEBI (MF) Regulations for the smooth and efficient functioning of the Scheme.

## What is HDFC Small Cap Fund?
HDFC Small Cap Fund is an open-ended equity mutual fund scheme that predominantly invests in small-cap companies. Small-cap companies typically represent emerging businesses with strong growth potential.
By investing in a diversified portfolio of small-cap stocks, the fund aims to identify companies with scalable business models and participate in their long-term growth journey.
Investment Objective is to provide long-term capital appreciation/ income by investing predominantly in small-cap companies. There is no assurance that the investment objective of the scheme will be achieved.
Source: https://www.hdfcfund.com/explore/mutual-funds/hdfc-small-cap-fund/direct (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## Investment Strategy of HDFC Small Cap Fund
HDFC Small Cap Fund focuses on identifying high-quality small-cap companies with long-term growth potential trading at reasonable valuations.
The portfolio construction emphasizes:
Bottom-up stock selection based on company fundamentals
Focus on quality companies, with sound financial strength and reasonable return on equity
Diversification across sectors and industries
Invest at sensible valuations in companies trading at reasonable multiples

## Why invest in HDFC Small Cap Fund?
Untapped Universe: With more than 1000 companies beyond the top 250 by market cap, the small-cap space offers a wider scope for alpha generation.
Under-Researched = Opportunity: Many small-cap stocks are less tracked by analysts, allowing fund managers to identify mispriced winners early.
Potential Re-rating: As small companies grow, they attract institutional interest and better coverage, often leading to higher valuations.
Proven Strategy: The fund has a 17+ year track record of spotting scalable, quality small-cap businesses with sound management.
Smart Diversification: Adds potential for high-growth to your portfolio, complementing large and mid-cap exposure of investors
Source: https://www.hdfcfund.com/explore/mutual-funds/hdfc-small-cap-fund/direct (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## How to start investing in HDFC Small Cap Fund?
Investors can start investing in HDFC Small Cap Fund through the following steps:
Complete your KYC Keep PAN, Aadhaar and bank details ready.
Visit HDFC Mutual Fund Website / App Go to the fund page and click Invest Now.
Choose Investment Mode SIP - invest a fixed amount monthly Lump Sum - invest one time
Select Plan & Option Direct or Regular Plan Growth or IDCW option
Enter Amount & Payment Details Set SIP date or make payment online.
Confirm & Start Investing Your investment will be processed and units will be allotted.

Entry Load An entry load is a fee charged to investors when they purchase units of a Mutual Fund scheme. This fee was used to cover distribution and marketing expenses incurred by the Asset Management Company (AMC). Currently, Mutual Funds in India do not levy entry loads. NA TER TER The Total Expense Ration (TER) represents the costs for running and managing a Mutual Fund scheme. It is calculated as a percentage of the scheme"s average Net Asset Value (NAV). The daily NAV of a Mutual Fund is disclosed after deducting these expenses. Disclaimer Including Additional Expenses and Goods and Service Tax on Management Fees, if any. ## Exit Load
In respect of each purchase / switch-in of Units, an Exit Load of 1.00% is payable if Units are redeemed / switched out within 1 year from the date of allotment.

### How is HDFC Small Cap Fund different from a large-cap fund?
A small-cap fund invests primarily in smaller companies, whereas large-cap funds invest mainly in well-established companies with larger market capitalisation. Small-cap funds may offer higher growth potential but can also experience higher volatility.

## About HDFC Balanced Advantage Fund
An open ended Balanced Advantage Fund. This Fund aims to provide long term capital appreciation/income from a mix of equity and debt investments. Different asset classes exhibit different risk-return profile and relatively low correlation to each other as compared to investments within the same asset class. The fund manager will determine asset allocation between equity and debt depending on prevailing market and economic conditions. The debt-equity mix at any point of time will be a function of interest rates, equity valuations, medium to long term outlook of the asset classes and risk management, etc.
An open ended Balanced Advantage Fund.
This Fund aims to provide long term capital appreciation/income from a mix of equity and debt investments.
Different asset classes exhibit different risk-return profile and relatively low correlation to each other as compared to investments within the same asset class. The fund manager will determine asset allocation between equity and debt depending on prevailing market and economic conditions.
The debt-equity mix at any point of time will be a function of interest rates, equity valuations, medium to long term outlook of the asset classes and risk management, etc.

This fee was used to cover distribution and marketing expenses incurred by the Asset Management Company (AMC). Currently, Mutual Funds in India do not levy entry loads. NA TER TER The Total Expense Ration (TER) represents the costs for running and managing a Mutual Fund scheme. It is calculated as a percentage of the scheme"s average Net Asset Value (NAV). The daily NAV of a Mutual Fund is disclosed after deducting these expenses. Disclaimer Including Additional Expenses and Goods and Service Tax on Management Fees, if any. ## About HDFC Balanced Advantage Fund
Entry Load An entry load is a fee charged to investors when they purchase units of a Mutual Fund scheme. This fee was used to cover distribution and marketing expenses incurred by the Asset Management Company (AMC). Currently, Mutual Funds in India do not levy entry loads. NA TER TER The Total Expense Ration (TER) represents the costs for running and managing a Mutual Fund scheme. It is calculated as a percentage of the scheme"s average Net Asset Value (NAV). The daily NAV of a Mutual Fund is disclosed after deducting these expenses. Disclaimer Including Additional Expenses and Goods and Service Tax on Management Fees, if any. ### Can I invest in SIP and Lump Sum of HDFC Balanced Advantage Fund ?
Yes, SIP as well as Lump Sum investments are permitted with the minimum application amount being ₹100. Any additional purchase must have a minimum amount of ₹100. The SIP and Lumpsum are available in direct and regular plan with both Growth and IDCW option.
Source: https://www.hdfcfund.com/explore/mutual-funds/hdfc-balanced-advantage-fund/direct (retrieved 2026-09-30)

### How to Invest in HDFC Balanced Advantage Fund?
Investors can apply for their transactions requests either offline or electronically using the relevant application / transaction request forms available on our website or at any of our Officials Points of Acceptance. The application form/transaction slip for subscription/ redemption/ switches can be submitted at our Official Points of Acceptances whose addresses are available on the website of the AMC. These include:
AMC / RTA"s branches i.e. Investor Services Centres*
HDFC MF website and App/ RTA website for Investors to transact
MFSS/BSE StAR MF/NMF II platforms of the Stock Exchanges(s)
Authorized Points of Service of MF Utilities India Private Limited (MFUI)
Channel partners/ Distributors/ RIAs/ Portfolio Managers/Execution Only Platforms (EOPs) who have tied up with the AMC
MF Central
*Note: Business Centres i.e. Sales offices of HDFC AMC are not Official Points of Acceptance of transactions. Please refer Scheme Documents above for more details
Source: https://www.hdfcfund.com/explore/mutual-funds/hdfc-balanced-advantage-fund/direct (retrieved 2026-09-30)

block_kind    : faq
chunk_index   : 47   token_count: 163   chars: 13184-14119
retrieved_at  : 2026-09-30
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### How to Redeem HDFC Balanced Advantage Fund?
Units of the scheme can be redeemed/switched out on any Business day at NAV based prices. For Redemption (including switch-out) applications • In respect of valid applications received upto 3 p.m. on a Business Day by the Fund, same day's closing NAV shall be applicable. • In respect of valid applications received after 3 p.m. on a Business Day by the Fund, the closing NAV of the next Business Day shall be applicable. Transactions through online facilities / electronic modes: The time of transaction done through various online facilities / electronic modes offered by the AMC, for the purpose of determining the applicability of NAV, would be the time when the request for purchase / sale / switch of units is received in the servers of AMC/RTA. The AMC has the right to amend cut off timings subject to SEBI (MF) Regulations for the smooth and efficient functioning of the Scheme.
Source: https://www.hdfcfund.com/explore/mutual-funds/hdfc-balanced-advantage-fund/direct (retrieved 2026-09-30)

## FAQs
Units of the scheme can be redeemed/switched out on any Business day at NAV based prices. For Redemption (including switch-out) applications • In respect of valid applications received upto 3 p.m. on a Business Day by the Fund, same day's closing NAV shall be applicable. • In respect of valid applications received after 3 p.m. on a Business Day by the Fund, the closing NAV of the next Business Day shall be applicable.
Transactions through online facilities / electronic modes:
The time of transaction done through various online facilities / electronic modes offered by the AMC, for the purpose of determining the applicability of NAV, would be the time when the request for purchase / sale / switch of units is received in the servers of AMC/RTA. The AMC has the right to amend cut off timings subject to SEBI (MF) Regulations for the smooth and efficient functioning of the Scheme.

# Request Statement
We offer you the convenience of having your account statement sent to your email id registered with us. Please enter your folio number in the box provided below and choose the option in which you wish to receive your account statement.
Pursuant to SEBI guidelines, with effect from October 1, 2011, an allotment confirmation specifying the units allotted shall be sent by way of email and/or SMS within 5 Business Days of the initial investment transaction to the Unit holders who have registered their e-mail address and/or mobile number.
Thereafter, a Consolidated Account Statement (CAS) shall be sent to the Unit holder in whose folio transactions have taken place during that month, on or before 10th of the succeeding month by mail/ email as applicable.
In case of specific request received from investors, Mutual Fund will provide an account statement to the investors within 5 Business Days from the receipt of such request.
Click here to view the addendum Click here for FAQ's

for FAQ's

Account statement by SMS
SMS to be sent as : >CAMS H SOA <Folio Number without check digit> <password> to 56767, e.g.CAMS H SOA 123456 ab785z
Response : Will either return an SMS confirmation that an account statement will be emailed; or in the event of error, will report the appropriate error.
Account statement through IVR
Source: https://www.hdfcfund.com/services/additional-info/request-statement (retrieved 2026-09-30)
